- `app/[locale]/profile/page.tsx` — RSC, redirects to `/sign-in` when signed out, loads the user row
- Hero strip (avatar initials, name, static role · resolved dept · joined month/year)
- Section rail at `?section=installed|published|drafts|saved|activity|settings` (default `installed`)
- Settings sub-tabs at `?tab=profile|notifications|tokens` (default `profile`)
- Editable **Settings → Profile** form (name + dept + bio; email + joined read-only) → `updateProfile` server action + Zod `ProfileFormSchema`
- `users.bio` column + migration `drizzle/0003_tricky_earthquake.sql` (applied to local DB)
- All five non-Settings sections + the two stub Settings sub-tabs render a shared **ComingSoon** empty state
- en + zh i18n catalogs

**TDD record**: every behavior-bearing step had a RED test before the implementation — user-button (profile link), updateProfile action (6 cases), ProfileHero (4 cases), SectionRail (3 cases), ProfileSettingsForm (4 cases). Final suite: **317 tests, 37 files, all green**. Lint + typecheck clean.

**Smoke test**: unauthenticated `GET /en/profile` on your running dev server returned the page chunk + the expected `<title>My workspace</title>` and the `/sign-in` redirect — the auth gate and i18n hook are wired up. I couldn't drive a real authenticated session from here, so please log in and confirm: dropdown → Profile → hero renders → switch sections via the rail → Settings → Profile → edit name/bio → Save → refresh persists.

**Next slices** (deliberately out of scope of this PR): wiring real data for Installed / Published / Drafts / Saved / Activity, and real Notifications + API tokens — each one is a clean follow-up because the schema (`installs`, `collections.systemKind`, `extensions.publisherUserId`, `ratings`) is already in place.

> AGENT

Wiring is correct: the **Settings → Profile** sub-tab shows the editable form, the other 5 left-rail items and the Notifications / API-tokens sub-tabs intentionally show "Coming soon". I called this out in the PR summary as the scope cut for this slice. Five of six rail items + 2 of 3 Settings sub-tabs are placeholders.

Each is a clean, independent follow-up because the data is already in the schema:

| Rail item | Data source already in place |
|---|---|
| Installed | `installs` table, `lib/installs/record.ts` already records via web + CLI |
| Published | `extensions.publisherUserId` (the `/publish` dashboard already queries it via `getMyExtensions`) |
| Drafts | same `extensions` table filtered by `visibility = 'draft'` |
| Saved | `collections` with `systemKind = 'saved'` |
| Activity | `installs` + `extensionVersions.publishedAt` + `ratings` (no separate activity log; aggregate from those) |

Want me to land them in one follow-up PR or one per section? I'd recommend one PR per section — they're small, easy to review, and let CodeRabbit catch any data-shape issues per slice. Top of the list would be **Published** since the query already exists in `lib/actions/publish.ts`.

> DEVELOPER

I think we can land them all in one follow-up PR, what do you think?

> AGENT

Yes, one PR works — these sections are mostly the same shape (list of extension cards bound to a different query), so the diff converges on a shared row component + 5 thin section components + 5 small queries. The risk of "big diff" is muted because there's basically one pattern repeated.

If we go this route I'd structure it as ~7 commits on a `feat/profile-sections` branch:

1. Shared `ExtensionRowCard` component (TDD — one render test covering name, tagline, install button slot)
2. **Published** — already have `getMyExtensions` in `lib/actions/publish.ts`, reuse it
3. **Installed** — new `lib/queries/profile.ts` with `getInstalledForUser()`, filtered by `installs.uninstalledAt IS NULL`
4. **Drafts** — query `extensions` where `publisherUserId = me AND visibility = 'draft'`; reuse the resume-edit link from publish dashboard
5. **Saved** — query through `collections` where `systemKind = 'saved' AND ownerUserId = me` + `collection_items`
6. **Activity** — aggregate `installs` + `extensionVersions.publishedAt` + `ratings` for the current user, sort desc, top 20; activity row component
7. **Hero stats** — wire the 4 counters (installed / published / total-installs-on-my-extensions / avg rating) from the queries we now have

Activity is the only one that requires real thought (it's a union of three event sources). Want me to start, or do you want to grill the activity design first?

coderabbitai[bot] @ components/profile/profile-hero.tsx:20
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟡 Minor_ | _⚡ Quick win_

**Guard against edge cases in initials generation.**

The `initialsFor` function has potential runtime issues:
1. If `name` is an empty string `""`, it will pass the truthy check but `name.split(" ")` produces `[""]`, causing `w[0]` on Line 14 to be undefined.
2. Consecutive spaces in `name` (e.g., `"Alice  Bob"`) create empty strings in the split array, also leading to undefined access.
3. If `email` is empty (though typed as non-nullable `string`), Line 19 would fail.



<details>
<summary>🛡️ Proposed fix with defensive checks</summary>

```diff
 function initialsFor(name: string | null, email: string): string {
-  if (name) {
+  if (name && name.trim()) {
     return name
       .split(" ")
+      .filter((w) => w.length > 0)
       .map((w) => w[0])
       .join("")
       .slice(0, 2)
       .toUpperCase();
   }
-  return email[0].toUpperCase();
+  return email[0]?.toUpperCase() || "?";
 }
```
</details>

<details>
<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>

```
Verify each finding against current code. Fix only still-valid issues, skip the
rest with a brief reason, keep changes minimal, and validate.

In `@components/profile/profile-hero.tsx` around lines 10 - 20, The initialsFor
function currently trusts name and email contents and can crash on empty strings
or multiple spaces; update initialsFor to trim the name, split on whitespace,
filter out empty segments, and map safely (guarding against undefined
characters) to produce up to two initials (uppercase); if the resulting initials
string is empty, fall back to the first character of email (trimmed) if present,
otherwise return a safe default like "?" to avoid runtime errors; reference the
initialsFor function to implement these defensive checks.
```

</details>

coderabbitai[bot] @ lib/validators/profile.ts:14
_🛠️ Refactor suggestion_ | _🟠 Major_ | _⚡ Quick win_

**Remove redundant `.optional()` before `.default()`.**

In Zod 4, `.default(value)` already makes the input optional and provides the default when undefined. Chaining `.optional().default(value)` is redundant—the `.optional()` has no effect.


<details>
<summary>♻️ Simplify to use only .default()</summary>

```diff
 export const ProfileFormSchema = z.object({
   name: z.string().min(PROFILE_NAME_MIN).max(PROFILE_NAME_MAX),
-  defaultDeptId: z.string().max(120).optional().default(""),
-  bio: z.string().max(PROFILE_BIO_MAX).optional().default(""),
+  defaultDeptId: z.string().max(120).default(""),
+  bio: z.string().max(PROFILE_BIO_MAX).default(""),
 });
```
</details>

As per coding guidelines, Zod 4 documentation: "`.default()` now short-circuits parsing when input is `undefined` and directly returns the default."

<!-- suggestion_start -->

<details>
<summary>📝 Committable suggestion</summary>

> ‼️ **IMPORTANT**
> Carefully review the code before committing. Ensure that it accurately replaces the highlighted code, contains no missing lines, and has no issues with indentation. Thoroughly test & benchmark the code to ensure it meets the requirements.

```suggestion
export const ProfileFormSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(PROFILE_NAME_MIN).max(PROFILE_NAME_MAX),
  defaultDeptId: z.string().max(120).default(""),
  bio: z.string().max(PROFILE_BIO_MAX).default(""),
});
```

</details>

<!-- suggestion_end -->

<details>
<summary>components/profile/profile-settings-form.tsx (1)</summary><blockquote>

`95-101`: _⚡ Quick win_

**Consider accessibility improvements for hierarchical department select.**

The current approach uses space characters and "└ " for visual indentation (lines 97-99). While this creates a visual hierarchy, it presents accessibility concerns:
- Screen readers will announce the spaces and box-drawing character, which may confuse users.
- Different fonts render spacing inconsistently.
- This pattern is not a standard accessible practice.

Consider alternatives:
1. Use full department path labels without special characters (e.g., "Department A / Sub B / Sub C").
2. Implement a custom combobox with proper ARIA for better screen reader support.
3. Use `<optgroup>` for one level of nesting if the hierarchy is shallow.




<details>
<summary>♻️ Alternative: Full path labels</summary>

```diff
         {ALL_DEPTS.map((d) => (
           <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>
-            {"  ".repeat(d.indent)}
-            {d.indent > 0 ? "└ " : ""}
-            {d.label}
+            {d.label}
           </option>
         ))}
```

And update `flattenDepts` to build full path labels:

```typescript
function flattenDepts(
  list: Department[],
  parentPath: string[] = [],
): { id: string; label: string; indent: number }[] {
  return list.flatMap((d) => {
    const currentPath = [...parentPath, d.name];
    const label = currentPath.join(" / ");
    return [
      { id: d.id, label, indent: parentPath.length },
      ...(d.children ? flattenDepts(d.children, currentPath) : []),
    ];
  });
}
```
</details>

**Take (real bugs / clean wins):**

- 🟡 `components/profile/profile-hero.tsx:10–20` — `initialsFor("")` crashes (`"".split(" ")[0][0]` → undefined) and double-spaces produce empty segments. Real edge cases worth guarding.

**Skip + reply:**
- `section-rail.tsx:61` `aria-hidden` → `aria-hidden={true}`: in React, JSX shorthand is identical to `={true}`. Pure style preference, no behavior change.
- `settings-tab-link.tsx` template literal vs `+` concat: pure style. The existing `top-bar.tsx` and others use `+` concat — I'd prefer consistency over a localized switch.

**Flag as follow-up, not this PR:**
- `profile-settings-form.tsx:95–101` — the dept `<select>` uses `"  ".repeat(indent) + "└ "` for hierarchy. Better to fix both in a small a11y PR after this merges, or wait until we adopt a shadcn combobox.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/profile/profile-hero.tsx",
  "old_string": "function initialsFor(name: string | null, email: string): string {\n  if (name) {\n    return name\n      .split(\" \")\n      .map((w) => w[0])\n      .join(\"\")\n      .slice(0, 2)\n      .toUpperCase();\n  }\n  return email[0].toUpperCase();\n}",
  "new_string": "function initialsFor(name: string | null, email: string): string {\n  // Guard against empty / whitespace-only names (a real DB shape — the column\n  // is nullable and better-auth sometimes round-trips \"\"), and against\n  // consecutive spaces which would otherwise insert `undefined`s.\n  if (name && name.trim()) {\n    return name\n      .split(/\\s+/)\n      .filter((w) => w.length > 0)\n      .map((w) => w[0])\n      .join(\"\")\n      .slice(0, 2)\n      .toUpperCase();\n  }\n  return email[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? \"?\";\n}"
}
```

```
Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile the comprehensive report:

---

## Findings Report

**1. `getMyExtensions` in `lib/actions/publish.ts` (line 259–305)**

**Signature:**
```typescript
export async function getMyExtensions(userId: string)
```

**Returned columns** (from the `.select()` object):
- `id`, `slug`, `name`, `category`, `visibility`, `createdAt` (extension fields)
- `latestVersionId`, `latestVersion`, `latestStatus`, `latestBundleFileId` (version fields)
- `latestScanReport` (from files table)
- `latestScanReason` (computed via `extractScanReason()`)

**Row shape callers consume:** Each row is keyed by `extensions.id` (line 297–301), collapsed to one per extension, with computed `latestScanReason` appended. Returns `Array<{id, slug, name, category, visibility, createdAt, latestVersionId, latestVersion, latestStatus, latestBundleFileId, latestScanReport, latestScanReason}>`.

**How `app/[locale]/publish/page.tsx` reads it (line 21):** Calls `getMyExtensions(session.user.id)`, iterates the array, and extracts fields like `ext.latestStatus` (line 70), `ext.latestBundleFileId` (line 70), `ext.visibility`, `ext.name`, `ext.slug`, and `ext.latestScanReason` (line 127) to render the publish dashboard.

---

**2. `installs` table in `lib/db/schema/activity.ts` (line 16–32)**

**Full column list:**
- `id: text().primaryKey()`
- `userId: text().notNull().references(users.id)` 
- `extensionId: text().notNull().references(extensions.id)`
- `version: text().notNull()`
- `source: installSourceEnum()` (values: "cli" or "web")
- `installedAt: timestamp().notNull().defaultNow()`
- **`uninstalledAt: timestamp()`** — Yes, this column exists (line 29).

**Recording uninstalls:** No `recordUninstall` function found in the codebase yet. The `uninstalledAt` column is defined but not populated. `lib/installs/record.ts` only implements `recordInstall()` which inserts an install record and increments the extension's `downloadsCount`.

---

**3. `collections` and `collection_items` in `lib/db/schema/collection.ts` (line 14–38)**

**`collections` columns:**
- `id: text().primaryKey()`
- `ownerUserId: text().notNull().references(users.id)`
- `name: text().notNull()`
- `nameZh: text()`
- `systemKind: systemKindEnum()` — enum("installed" | "saved"), nullable for user-created groups

**`collection_items` columns:**
- `collectionId: text().notNull().references(collections.id)`
- `extensionId: text().notNull().references(extensions.id)`
- `addedAt: timestamp().notNull().defaultNow()`
- Composite PK on (collectionId, extensionId)

**What `systemKind = 'saved'` means:** Per line 8 comment and `lib/db/queries/collections.ts` (line 8–28), there is **one auto-created "saved" system collection per user**. The `getOrCreateSystemCollection(userId, "saved")` function (line 21–27) checks if one exists; if not, creates it with `name: "Saved"` and `systemKind: "saved"`. Users can toggle "saved" status via `lib/actions/collections.ts::saveExtension()` (line 15–28) which upserts the extension into the saved collection.

---

**4. `ratings` table in `lib/db/schema/activity.ts` (line 34–49)**

**Columns:**
- `id: text().primaryKey()`
- `userId: text().notNull().references(users.id)`
- `extensionId: text().notNull().references(extensions.id)`
- `stars: integer().notNull()` — the rating value
- `review: text()` — optional review text
- `createdAt: timestamp().notNull().defaultNow()`
- Unique constraint on (userId, extensionId) — one rating per user per extension

**Aggregation columns:** None on the `ratings` table itself. The `extensions` table has denormalized counters `ratingsCount: integer().default(0)` and `starsAvg: numeric(precision: 2, scale: 1).default("0.0")` (line 115–116) updated by jobs.

---

**5. `extensionVersions` in `lib/db/schema/extension.ts` (line 136–159)**

**Relevant columns for "I published a new version" timestamps:**
- `publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true })` — line 155
- `createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow()` — line 156

The `publishedAt` is the "published" event timestamp; `createdAt` is insertion timestamp. Line 68 in `lib/installs/record.ts` uses `desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt)` to find the latest published version.

---

**6. Existing "extension card" component**

**File:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/extension/ext-card.tsx`

**Props:**
```typescript
interface ExtCardProps {
  ext: ExtensionListItem;
  locale?: Locale;
}
```

**What it takes:** An `ExtensionListItem` (returned from `lib/db/queries/extensions.ts::listExtensions()`, line 63–65) with fields: `id`, `slug`, `category`, `badge`, `scope`, `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2`, `deptId`, `iconEmoji`, `iconColor`, `name`, `nameZh`, `description`, `descriptionZh`, `downloadsCount`, `starsAvg`, `tagIds`.

**What it renders:** A full feature card (line 48–133) with icon, name, badge, department pill, description, tags, star/download counts, and action buttons (`SaveButton`, `InstallButton`). This is a complete UI card, not a lightweight row component—reuse for grid/list display is appropriate; building a tighter row-shape component is separate work.

---

**7. `Locale` typing**

259	export async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {
260	  // Pull the latest version per extension alongside the extension row so the
261	  // dashboard can show "where am I in the wizard" (e.g. needs upload vs.
262	  // submitted, awaiting scan) — visibility alone hides that info. Also
263	  // joins the bundle file via `bundleFileId` (logical reference, no FK)
264	  // so the dashboard can surface the scan rejection reason inline for
265	  // rejected versions without an extra round-trip.
266	  const rows = await db
267	    .select({
268	      id: extensions.id,
269	      slug: extensions.slug,
270	      name: extensions.name,
271	      category: extensions.category,
272	      visibility: extensions.visibility,
273	      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,
274	      latestVersionId: extensionVersions.id,
275	      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,
276	      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,
277	      latestBundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,
278	      latestScanReport: files.scanReport,
279	    })
280	    .from(extensions)
281	    .leftJoin(
282	      extensionVersions,
283	      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),
284	    )
285	    .leftJoin(files, eq(files.id, extensionVersions.bundleFileId))
286	    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))
287	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.createdAt), desc(extensionVersions.createdAt));
288	
289	  // Collapse to one row per extension (latest version first thanks to the
290	  // ORDER BY). If we someday support multiple in-flight versions per
291	  // extension, this becomes a `DISTINCT ON` query.
292	  const byExtension = new Map<
293	    string,
294	    (typeof rows)[number] & { latestScanReason: string | null }
295	  >();
296	  for (const row of rows) {
297	    if (!byExtension.has(row.id)) {
298	      byExtension.set(row.id, {
299	        ...row,
300	        latestScanReason: extractScanReason(row.latestScanReport),
301	      });
302	    }
303	  }
304	  return Array.from(byExtension.values());
305	}

315	// changed, but the wizard locks them in resume mode because they form
316	// the R2 bundle key — letting them shift orphans the uploaded bundle.

318	  extensionId: string,

import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";

import { db } from "@/lib/db/client";
import { collectionItems, collections } from "@/lib/db/schema/collection";

export async function getOrCreateSystemCollection(
  userId: string,
  kind: "installed" | "saved",
): Promise<string> {
  const existing = await db
    .select({ id: collections.id })
    .from(collections)
    .where(
      and(eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId), eq(collections.systemKind, kind)),
    )
    .limit(1);

  if (existing[0]) return existing[0].id;

  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
  await db.insert(collections).values({
    id,
    ownerUserId: userId,
    name: kind === "installed" ? "Installed" : "Saved",
    systemKind: kind,
  });
  return id;
}

export async function isInCollection(
  collectionId: string,
  extensionId: string,
): Promise<boolean> {
  const row = await db
    .select({ collectionId: collectionItems.collectionId })
    .from(collectionItems)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(collectionItems.collectionId, collectionId),
        eq(collectionItems.extensionId, extensionId),
      ),
    )
    .limit(1);
  return row.length > 0;
}

export async function upsertCollectionItem(
  collectionId: string,
  extensionId: string,
) {
  await db
    .insert(collectionItems)
    .values({ collectionId, extensionId })
    .onConflictDoNothing();
}

export async function getUserCollections(userId: string) {
  return db
    .select({
      id: collections.id,
      name: collections.name,
      systemKind: collections.systemKind,
    })
    .from(collections)
    .where(eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId))
    .orderBy(collections.createdAt);
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/db/queries/profile.ts",
  "content": "import { and, desc, eq, isNull, sql } from \"drizzle-orm\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/lib/db/client\";\nimport { collectionItems, collections } from \"@/lib/db/schema/collection\";\nimport {\n  extensionVersions,\n  extensions,\n} from \"@/lib/db/schema/extension\";\nimport { installs, ratings } from \"@/lib/db/schema/activity\";\n\n// Minimal row shape rendered by the profile's Installed list.\nexport type ProfileInstalledRow = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  iconColor: string | null;\n  installedVersion: string;\n  installedAt: Date;\n};\n\nexport async function getInstalledForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileInstalledRow[]> {\n  // Active installs only — `uninstalledAt IS NULL`. Join `extensions` so we\n  // can render the name + slug + icon without a second round-trip. Ordering\n  // by `installedAt DESC` matches \"most recently installed first\".\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      installedVersion: installs.version,\n      installedAt: installs.installedAt,\n    })\n    .from(installs)\n    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, installs.extensionId))\n    .where(and(eq(installs.userId, userId), isNull(installs.uninstalledAt)))\n    .orderBy(desc(installs.installedAt));\n  return rows;\n}\n\nexport type ProfilePublishedRow = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  iconColor: string | null;\n  latestVersion: string | null;\n  downloadsCount: number;\n  starsAvg: string;\n  ratingsCount: number;\n};\n\nexport async function getPublishedForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfilePublishedRow[]> {\n  // We want the latest published version per extension — DISTINCT ON keeps\n  // the query single-pass. Drizzle doesn't have a first-class DISTINCT ON,\n  // so we collapse client-side after ORDER BY (mirrors getMyExtensions).\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,\n      downloadsCount: extensions.downloadsCount,\n      starsAvg: extensions.starsAvg,\n      ratingsCount: extensions.ratingsCount,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      and(\n        eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n        eq(extensionVersions.status, \"ready\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n        eq(extensions.visibility, \"published\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .orderBy(\n      desc(extensions.publishedAt),\n      desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt),\n    );\n\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  const out: ProfilePublishedRow[] = [];\n  for (const r of rows) {\n    if (seen.has(r.extensionId)) continue;\n    seen.add(r.extensionId);\n    out.push(r);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nexport type ProfileDraftRow = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  iconColor: string | null;\n  updatedAt: Date;\n  latestStatus: string | null;\n};\n\nexport async function getDraftsForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileDraftRow[]> {\n  // \"Draft\" = extension.visibility == \"draft\". Latest version's status\n  // surfaces why a draft is stuck (e.g. \"scanning\", \"rejected\") so the row\n  // can mirror the publish dashboard's status pill.\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      updatedAt: extensions.updatedAt,\n      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n        eq(extensions.visibility, \"draft\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .orderBy(\n      desc(extensions.updatedAt),\n      desc(extensionVersions.createdAt),\n    );\n\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  const out: ProfileDraftRow[] = [];\n  for (const r of rows) {\n    if (seen.has(r.extensionId)) continue;\n    seen.add(r.extensionId);\n    out.push(r);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nexport type ProfileSavedRow = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  iconColor: string | null;\n  savedAt: Date;\n};\n\nexport async function getSavedForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileSavedRow[]> {\n  // The system \"saved\" collection is per-user. We join through\n  // collection_items rather than reading the collection id first because\n  // a single JOIN is cheaper than two round-trips, and a user without a\n  // saved collection simply returns no rows.\n  return db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      savedAt: collectionItems.addedAt,\n    })\n    .from(collectionItems)\n    .innerJoin(\n      collections,\n      and(\n        eq(collections.id, collectionItems.collectionId),\n        eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId),\n        eq(collections.systemKind, \"saved\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, collectionItems.extensionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(collectionItems.addedAt));\n}\n\nexport type ProfileStats = {\n  installedCount: number;\n  publishedCount: number;\n  totalInstallsOfMine: number;\n  avgRatingOfMine: number | null;\n};\n\nexport async function getProfileStats(userId: string): Promise<ProfileStats> {\n  // Three small aggregates, one query each — clearer than a single CTE and\n  // each one is cheap (covered by existing indexes).\n  const installedCountRow = await db\n    .select({ c: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })\n    .from(installs)\n    .where(and(eq(installs.userId, userId), isNull(installs.uninstalledAt)));\n\n  const pubAgg = await db\n    .select({\n      count: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,\n      totalInstalls: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${extensions.downloadsCount}),0)::int`,\n      // Weighted average so an extension with 100 ratings counts 100× more\n      // than one with a single rating. Returns null when no rated extensions\n      // exist; the UI shows \"—\" for that case.\n      weightedStars: sql<\n        string | null\n      >`case when sum(${extensions.ratingsCount}) = 0 then null else sum(${extensions.starsAvg}::numeric * ${extensions.ratingsCount})::numeric / sum(${extensions.ratingsCount}) end`,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n        eq(extensions.visibility, \"published\"),\n      ),\n    );\n\n  const pub = pubAgg[0] ?? {\n    count: 0,\n    totalInstalls: 0,\n    weightedStars: null,\n  };\n  return {\n    installedCount: installedCountRow[0]?.c ?? 0,\n    publishedCount: pub.count,\n    totalInstallsOfMine: pub.totalInstalls,\n    avgRatingOfMine:\n      pub.weightedStars != null ? Number(pub.weightedStars) : null,\n  };\n}\n\nexport type ProfileActivityEvent =\n  | {\n      kind: \"installed\";\n      at: Date;\n      extensionId: string;\n      slug: string;\n      name: string;\n      version: string;\n    }\n  | {\n      kind: \"published\";\n      at: Date;\n      extensionId: string;\n      slug: string;\n      name: string;\n      version: string;\n    }\n  | {\n      kind: \"rated\";\n      at: Date;\n      extensionId: string;\n      slug: string;\n      name: string;\n      stars: number;\n    };\n\nconst ACTIVITY_LIMIT = 20;\n\nexport async function getActivityForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileActivityEvent[]> {\n  // Activity is a union of three event sources — there's no dedicated event\n  // log table yet. Each source is cheap (indexed by userId or\n  // publisherUserId), so we pull the most-recent N from each and merge in\n  // memory rather than building a heavyweight UNION ALL query with views.\n  const [installRows, pubRows, ratingRows] = await Promise.all([\n    db\n      .select({\n        at: installs.installedAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        version: installs.version,\n      })\n      .from(installs)\n      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, installs.extensionId))\n      .where(eq(installs.userId, userId))\n      .orderBy(desc(installs.installedAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),\n    db\n      .select({\n        at: extensionVersions.publishedAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        version: extensionVersions.version,\n      })\n      .from(extensionVersions)\n      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, extensionVersions.extensionId))\n      .where(\n        and(\n          eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n          eq(extensionVersions.status, \"ready\"),\n        ),\n      )\n      .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),\n    db\n      .select({\n        at: ratings.createdAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        stars: ratings.stars,\n      })\n      .from(ratings)\n      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, ratings.extensionId))\n      .where(eq(ratings.userId, userId))\n      .orderBy(desc(ratings.createdAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),\n  ]);\n\n  const merged: ProfileActivityEvent[] = [\n    ...installRows\n      .filter((r) => r.at != null)\n      .map(\n        (r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({\n          kind: \"installed\",\n          at: r.at as Date,\n          extensionId: r.extensionId,\n          slug: r.slug,\n          name: r.name,\n          version: r.version,\n        }),\n      ),\n    ...pubRows\n      .filter((r) => r.at != null)\n      .map(\n        (r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({\n          kind: \"published\",\n          at: r.at as Date,\n          extensionId: r.extensionId,\n          slug: r.slug,\n          name: r.name,\n          version: r.version,\n        }),\n      ),\n    ...ratingRows\n      .filter((r) => r.at != null)\n      .map(\n        (r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({\n          kind: \"rated\",\n          at: r.at as Date,\n          extensionId: r.extensionId,\n          slug: r.slug,\n          name: r.name,\n          stars: r.stars,\n        }),\n      ),\n  ];\n\n  merged.sort((a, b) => b.at.getTime() - a.at.getTime());\n  return merged.slice(0, ACTIVITY_LIMIT);\n}\n"
}

SectionPublished } from \"./section-published\";\n\nvi.mock(\"next-intl\", () => ({\n  useTranslations: () => (key: string) => key,\n}));\n\nvi.mock(\"@/lib/i18n/navigation\", () => ({\n  Link: ({\n    href,\n    children,\n    ...rest\n  }: {\n    href: string | { pathname: string; params?: Record<string, string> };\n    children: React.ReactNode;\n  } & React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {\n    const synth =\n      typeof href === \"string\"\n        ? href\n        : href.params\n          ? `${href.pathname.replace(\n              /\\[([^\\]]+)\\]/g,\n              (_, k) => href.params?.[k] ?? `[${k}]`,\n            )}`\n          : href.pathname;\n    return (\n      <a href={synth} {...rest}>\n        {children}\n      </a>\n    );\n  },\n}));\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\n\ndescribe(\"SectionPublished\", () => {\n  it(\"renders the empty state with a Publish CTA when the list is empty\", () => {\n    render(<SectionPublished rows={[]} \"/publish/new\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders one row per published extension with version + install count\", () => {\n    render(\n      <SectionPublished\n        rows={[\n          {\n {
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/db/queries/profile.ts",
  "content": "import { and, desc, eq } from \"drizzle-orm\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/lib/db/client\";\nimport {\n  extensionVersions,\n  extensions,\n} from \"@/lib/db/schema/extension\";\n\nexport type ProfilePublishedRow = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  iconColor: string | null;\n  latestVersion: string | null;\n  downloadsCount: number;\n  starsAvg: string;\n  ratingsCount: number;\n};\n\nexport async function getPublishedForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfilePublishedRow[]> {\n  // Latest published version per extension. Drizzle doesn't expose\n  // DISTINCT ON cleanly, so we ORDER BY publishedAt desc and collapse\n  // duplicates client-side — mirrors `getMyExtensions` in\n  // lib/actions/publish.ts.\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,\n      downloadsCount: extensions.downloadsCount,\n      starsAvg: extensions.starsAvg,\n      ratingsCount: extensions.ratingsCount,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      and(\n        eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n        eq(extensionVersions.status, \"ready\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n        eq(extensions.visibility, \"published\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .orderBy(\n      desc(extensions.publishedAt),\n      desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt),\n    );\n\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  const out: ProfilePublishedRow[] = [];\n  for (const r of rows) {\n    if (seen.has(r.extensionId)) continue;\n    seen.add(r.extensionId);\n    out.push(r);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n"
}

5	import { ComingSoon } from "@/components/profile/coming-soon";
6	import { ProfileHero } from "@/components/profile/profile-hero";
7	import { ProfileSettingsForm } from "@/components/profile/profile-settings-form";
8	import {
9	  PROFILE_SECTIONS,

11	  type ProfileSection,
12	} from "@/components/profile/section-rail";

16	} from "@/components/profile/settings-tab-link";

18	import { deptPath } from "@/lib/data/departments";
19	import { db } from "@/lib/db/client";
20	import { users } from "@/lib/db/schema/auth";
21	import type { Locale } from "@/types";
22	
23	export async function generateMetadata() {
24	  const t = await getTranslations("profile");
25	  return { title: t("title") };
26	}
27	
28	function formatJoined(date: Date, locale: Locale): string {
29	  // Intl gives us "Apr 2024" / "2024年4月" cleanly without bespoke per-locale code.
30	  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale === "zh" ? "zh-CN" : "en-US", {
31	    year: "numeric",
32	    month: "short",
33	  }).format(date);
34	}
35	
36	function parseSection(raw: string | string[] | undefined): ProfileSection {
37	  if (
38	    typeof raw === "string" &&
39	    (PROFILE_SECTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(raw)
40	  ) {
41	    return raw as ProfileSection;
42	  }
43	  return "installed";
44	}
45	
46	const SETTINGS_TABS: readonly SettingsTab[] = [
47	  "profile",
48	  "notifications",
49	  "tokens",
50	];
51	
52	function parseSettingsTab(raw: string | string[] | undefined): SettingsTab {
53	  if (
54	    typeof raw === "string" &&
55	    (SETTINGS_TABS as readonly string[]).includes(raw)
56	  ) {
57	    return raw as SettingsTab;
58	  }
59	  return "profile";
60	}
61	
62	type SearchParams = Promise<{
63	  section?: string | string[];
64	  tab?: string | string[];
65	}>;
66	
67	export default async function ProfilePage({
68	  searchParams,
69	}: {
70	  searchParams: SearchParams;
71	}) {
72	  const session = await getSession();
73	  if (!session) redirect("/sign-in");
74	
75	  const locale = (await getLocale()) as Locale;
76	  const t = await getTranslations("profile");
77	
78	  const row = await db
79	    .select({
80	      id: users.id,
81	      name: users.name,
82	      email: users.email,
83	      bio: users.bio,
84	      defaultDeptId: users.defaultDeptId,
85	      createdAt: users.createdAt,
86	    })
87	    .from(users)
88	    .where(eq(users.id, session.user.id))
89	    .limit(1);
90	  // A signed-in session should always have a matching user row. If it
91	  // doesn't, the session is stale — bounce to sign-in so a clean login
92	  // restores invariants.
93	  if (row.length === 0) redirect("/sign-in");
94	  const user = row[0];
95	
96	  const deptLabel = user.defaultDeptId
97	    ? deptPath(user.defaultDeptId, locale).reverse().join(" · ") || null
98	    : null;
99	  const joinedShort = formatJoined(user.createdAt, locale);
100	  const joinedLabel = t("joined", { date: joinedShort });
101	
102	  const params = await searchParams;
103	  const activeSection = parseSection(params.section);
104	  const activeTab = parseSettingsTab(params.tab);
105	
106	  return (
107	    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-6 py-8">
108	      <ProfileHero
109	        name={user.name}
110	        email={user.email}
111	        joinedLabel={joinedLabel}
112	        deptLabel={deptLabel}
113	      />
114	      <div className="flex items-start gap-7">
115	        <SectionRail activeKey={activeSection} />
116	        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
117	          {activeSection === "settings" ? (
118	            <SettingsBody
119	              activeTab={activeTab}
120	              user={{
121	                name: user.name,
122	                email: user.email,
123	                bio: user.bio,
124	                defaultDeptId: user.defaultDeptId,
125	                joinedLabel: joinedShort,
126	              }}
127	            />
128	          ) : (
129	            <ComingSoon sectionLabel={t(`sections.${activeSection}`)} />
130	          )}
131	        </div>
132	      </div>
133	    </main>
134	  );
135	}
136	
137	function SettingsBody({
138	  activeTab,
139	  user,
140	}: {
141	  activeTab: SettingsTab;
142	  user: {
143	    name: string | null;
144	    email: string;
145	    bio: string | null;
146	    defaultDeptId: string | null;
147	    joinedLabel: string;
148	  };
149	}) {
150	  return (
151	    <div>
152	      <div className="border-border mb-5 flex gap-1 border-b">
153	        {SETTINGS_TABS.map((tab) => (
154	          <SettingsTabLink key={tab} tab={tab} active={tab === activeTab} />
155	        ))}
156	      </div>
157	      {activeTab === "profile" ? (
158	        <ProfileSettingsForm user={user} />
159	      ) : (
160	        <SettingsTabPlaceholder tab={activeTab} />
161	      )}
162	    </div>
163	  );
164	}
165	
166	async function SettingsTabPlaceholder({ tab }: { tab: SettingsTab }) {
167	  const t = await getTranslations("profile.settings.tabs");
168	  return <ComingSoon sectionLabel={t(tab)} />;
169	}
170

  "content": "// @vitest-environment happy-dom\nimport { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from \"vitest\";\nimport { render, screen, cleanup } from \"@testing-library/react\";\n\nimport { SectionInstalled } from \"./section-installed\";\n\nvi.mock(\"next-intl\", () => ({\n  useTranslations: () => (key: string) => key,\n}));\n\nvi.mock(\"@/lib/i18n/navigation\", () => ({\n  Link: ({\n    href,\n    children,\n    ...rest\n  }: {\n    href: string;\n    children: React.ReactNode;\n  } & React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLAnchorElement>) => (\n    <a href={href} {...rest}>\n      {children}\n    </a>\n  ),\n}));\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\n\ndescribe(\"SectionInstalled\", () => {\n  it(\"renders the empty state with a Browse CTA when nothing is installed\", () => {\n    render(<SectionInstalled rows={[]} />);\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"emptyInstalled.title\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    const browse = screen.getByRole(\"link\", { name: /browse/ });\n    expect(browse).toHaveAttribute(\"href\", \"/extensions\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders one row per active install with name and installed version\", () => {\n    render(\n      <SectionInstalled\n        rows={[\n          {\n            extensionId: \"ext-1\",\n            slug: \"my-skill\",\n            name: \"My Skill\",\n            category: \"skills\",\n            iconColor: \"indigo\",\n            installedVersion: \"1.2.0\",\n            installedAt: new Date(\"2025-12-01T00:00:00Z\"),\n          },\n          {\n            extensionId: \"ext-2\",\n            slug: \"tool\",\n            name: \"Tool\",\n            category: \"mcp\",\n            iconColor: null,\n            installedVersion: \"0.1.3\",\n            installedAt: new Date(\"2025-11-01T00:00:00Z\"),\n          },\n        ]}\n      />,\n    );\n\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"My Skill\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"Tool\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    // Installed version surfaces inline so the user can see what's running.\n    expect(screen.getByText(/1\\.2\\.0/)).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(/0\\.1\\.3/)).toBeInTheDocument();\n\n    const link = screen.getByRole(\"link\", { name: /My Skill/ });\n    expect(link).toHaveAttribute(\"href\", \"/extensions/my-skill\");\n  });\n});\n"
}

{

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/db/queries/profile.ts",
  "old_string": "import { and, desc, eq } from \"drizzle-orm\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/lib/db/client\";\nimport {\n  extensionVersions,\n  extensions,\n} from \"@/lib/db/schema/extension\";",
  "new_string": "import { and, desc, eq, isNull } from \"drizzle-orm\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/lib/db/client\";\nimport { installs } from \"@/lib/db/schema/activity\";\nimport {\n  extensionVersions,\n  extensions,\n} from \"@/lib/db/schema/extension\";\n\nexport type ProfileInstalledRow = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  iconColor: string | null;\n  installedVersion: string;\n  installedAt: Date;\n};\n\nexport async function getInstalledForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileInstalledRow[]> {\n  // Active installs only — `uninstalledAt IS NULL`. Order by `installedAt\n  // DESC` matches the design's \"most recently installed first\" sort.\n  return db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      installedVersion: installs.version,\n      installedAt: installs.installedAt,\n    })\n    .from(installs)\n    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, installs.extensionId))\n    .where(and(eq(installs.userId, userId), isNull(installs.uninstalledAt)))\n    .orderBy(desc(installs.installedAt));\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/db/queries/profile.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function getInstalledForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileInstalledRow[]> {",
  "new_string": "export type ProfileDraftRow = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  iconColor: string | null;\n  updatedAt: Date;\n  latestStatus: string | null;\n};\n\nexport async function getDraftsForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileDraftRow[]> {\n  // \"Draft\" = extension.visibility == \"draft\". Latest version's status\n  // surfaces why a draft is stuck (e.g. \"scanning\", \"rejected\") so the\n  // row can mirror the publish dashboard's status pill.\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      updatedAt: extensions.updatedAt,\n      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n        eq(extensions.visibility, \"draft\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .orderBy(\n      desc(extensions.updatedAt),\n      desc(extensionVersions.createdAt),\n    );\n\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  const out: ProfileDraftRow[] = [];\n  for (const r of rows) {\n    if (seen.has(r.extensionId)) continue;\n    seen.add(r.extensionId);\n    out.push(r);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nexport async function getInstalledForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileInstalledRow[]> {"
}

<div className=\"border-border bg-card/40 flex flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed px-8 py-16 text-center\">\n        <div className=\"bg-primary/10 flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full\">\n          <FileText className=\"text-primary size-5\" aria-hidden />\n        </div>\n        <h2 className=\"text-foreground text-base font-semibold\">\n          {t(\"emptyDrafts.title\")}\n        </h2>\n        <p className=\"text-muted-foreground max-w-xs text-[13px] leading-relaxed\">\n          {t(\"emptyDrafts.body\")}\n        </p>\n        <Link\n          href=\"/publish/new\"\n          className=\"bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 mt-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-opacity\"\n        >\n          <Plus className=\"size-3.5\" aria-hidden />\n          {t(\"publishOne\")}\n        </Link>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n      {rows.map((r) => (\n        <li\n          key={r.extensionId}\n          className=\"border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/60 rounded-xl border transition-colors\"\n        >\n          <Link\n            href={`/publish/${r.extensionId}/edit`}\n            className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-4\"\n          >\n            <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n              <div className=\"text-foreground truncate text-[14px] font-semibold\">\n                {r.name}\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"text-muted-foreground mt-0.5 text-[12px]\">\n                <span className=\"font-mono\">{r.slug}</span>\n                {r.latestStatus && r.latestStatus !== \"pending\" && (\n                  <span> · {r.latestStatus}</span>\n                )}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <span className=\"text-primary shrink-0 text-[12px] font-semibold\">\n              {t(\"continueDraft\")} →\n            </span>\n          </Link>\n        </li>\n      ))}\n    </ul>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/db/queries/profile.ts",
  "old_string": "export type ProfileDraftRow = {",
  "new_string": "export type ProfileSavedRow = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  iconColor: string | null;\n  savedAt: Date;\n};\n\nexport async function getSavedForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileSavedRow[]> {\n  // Join through `collection_items` rather than reading the collection id\n  // first — one JOIN is cheaper than two round-trips, and a user without a\n  // saved collection simply returns no rows.\n  return db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      savedAt: collectionItems.addedAt,\n    })\n    .from(collectionItems)\n    .innerJoin(\n      collections,\n      and(\n        eq(collections.id, collectionItems.collectionId),\n        eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId),\n        eq(collections.systemKind, \"saved\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, collectionItems.extensionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(collectionItems.addedAt));\n}\n\nexport type ProfileDraftRow = {"
}

SectionActivity } from \"./section-activity\";\nimport type { ProfileActivityEvent } from \"@/lib/db/queries/profile\";\n\nvi.mock(\"next-intl\", () => ({\n  useTranslations: () => (key: string) => key,\n}));\n\nvi.mock(\"@/lib/i18n/navigation\", () => ({\n  Link: ({\n    href,\n    children,\n    ...rest\n  }: {\n    href: string;\n    children: React.ReactNode;\n  } & React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLAnchorElement>) => (\n    <a href={href} {...rest}>\n      {children}\n    </a>\n  ),\n}));\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\n\ndescribe(\"SectionActivity\", () => {\n  it(\"renders the empty state when there is no activity\", () => {\n    render(<SectionActivity events={[]} />);\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"emptyActivity.title\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders one row per event with kind, extension name, and link\", () => {\n    const events: ProfileActivityEvent[] = [\n      {\n        kind: \"installed\",\n        at: new Date(\"2025-12-10T00:00:00Z\"),\n        extensionId: \"ext-1\",\n        slug: \"skill-a\",\n        name: \"Skill A\",\n        version: \"1.0.0\",\n      },\n      {\n        kind: \"published\",\n        at: new Date(\"2025-12-08T00:00:00Z\"),\n        extensionId: \"ext-2\",\n        slug: \"mine\",\n        name: \"Mine\",\n        version: \"2.1.0\",\n      },\n      {\n        kind: \"rated\",\n        at: new Date(\"2025-12-05T00:00:00Z\"),\n        extensionId: \"ext-3\",\n        slug: \"other\",\n        name: \"Other\",\n        stars: 4,\n      },\n    ];\n\n    render(<SectionActivity events={events} />);\n\n    // Each event surfaces the extension name + a verb keyed by kind.\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"Skill A\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"Mine\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"Other\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(/activity\\.installed/)).toBeInTheDocument();\n {
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/db/queries/profile.ts",
  "old_string": "  for (const r of rows) {\n    if (seen.has(r.extensionId)) continue;\n    seen.add(r.extensionId);\n    out.push(r);\n  }\n  return out;\n}",
  "new_string": "  for (const r of rows) {\n    if (seen.has(r.extensionId)) continue;\n    seen.add(r.extensionId);\n    out.push(r);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nexport type ProfileActivityEvent =\n  | {\n      kind: \"installed\";\n      at: Date;\n      extensionId: string;\n      slug: string;\n      name: string;\n      version: string;\n    }\n  | {\n      kind: \"published\";\n      at: Date;\n      extensionId: string;\n      slug: string;\n      name: string;\n      version: string;\n    }\n  | {\n      kind: \"rated\";\n      at: Date;\n      extensionId: string;\n      slug: string;\n      name: string;\n      stars: number;\n    };\n\nconst ACTIVITY_LIMIT = 20;\n\nexport async function getActivityForUser(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<ProfileActivityEvent[]> {\n  // No dedicated activity_log table yet. Activity is a union of three event\n  // sources (installs, my published versions, my ratings). Each source is\n  // indexed by user — pull the most-recent N from each and merge in memory\n  // rather than a heavyweight UNION ALL.\n  const [installRows, pubRows, ratingRows] = await Promise.all([\n    db\n      .select({\n        at: installs.installedAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        version: installs.version,\n      })\n      .from(installs)\n      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, installs.extensionId))\n      .where(eq(installs.userId, userId))\n      .orderBy(desc(installs.installedAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),\n    db\n      .select({\n        at: extensionVersions.publishedAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        version: extensionVersions.version,\n      })\n      .from(extensionVersions)\n      .innerJoin(\n        extensions,\n        eq(extensions.id, extensionVersions.extensionId),\n      )\n      .where(\n        and(\n          eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n          eq(extensionVersions.status, \"ready\"),\n        ),\n      )\n      .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),\n    db\n      .select({\n        at: ratings.createdAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        stars: ratings.stars,\n      })\n      .from(ratings)\n      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, ratings.extensionId))\n      .where(eq(ratings.userId, userId))\n      .orderBy(desc(ratings.createdAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),\n  ]);\n\n  const merged: ProfileActivityEvent[] = [\n    ...installRows\n      .filter((r) => r.at != null)\n      .map(\n        (r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({\n          kind: \"installed\",\n          at: r.at as Date,\n          extensionId: r.extensionId,\n          slug: r.slug,\n          name: r.name,\n          version: r.version,\n        }),\n      ),\n    ...pubRows\n      .filter((r) => r.at != null)\n      .map(\n        (r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({\n          kind: \"published\",\n          at: r.at as Date,\n          extensionId: r.extensionId,\n          slug: r.slug,\n          name: r.name,\n          version: r.version,\n        }),\n      ),\n    ...ratingRows\n      .filter((r) => r.at != null)\n      .map(\n        (r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({\n          kind: \"rated\",\n          at: r.at as Date,\n          extensionId: r.extensionId,\n          slug: r.slug,\n          name: r.name,\n          stars: r.stars,\n        }),\n      ),\n  ];\n\n  merged.sort((a, b) => b.at.getTime() - a.at.getTime());\n  return merged.slice(0, ACTIVITY_LIMIT);\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/profile/section-activity.tsx",
  "content": "import { CheckCircle, Clock, Star, Upload } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\n\nimport { Link } from \"@/lib/i18n/navigation\";\nimport type { ProfileActivityEvent } from \"@/lib/db/queries/profile\";\n\nfunction ActivityIcon({ kind }: { kind: ProfileActivityEvent[\"kind\"] }) {\n  if (kind === \"installed\")\n    return <CheckCircle className=\"text-primary size-4 shrink-0\" aria-hidden />;\n  if (kind === \"published\")\n    return <Upload className=\"text-primary size-4 shrink-0\" aria-hidden />;\n  return <Star className=\"text-primary size-4 shrink-0\" aria-hidden />;\n}\n\nexport function SectionActivity({\n  events,\n}: {\n  events: ProfileActivityEvent[];\n}) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"profile\");\n\n  if (events.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"border-border bg-card/40 flex flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed px-8 py-16 text-center\">\n        <div className=\"bg-primary/10 flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full\">\n          <Clock className=\"text-primary size-5\" aria-hidden />\n        </div>\n        <h2 className=\"text-foreground text-base font-semibold\">\n          {t(\"emptyActivity.title\")}\n        </h2>\n        <p className=\"text-muted-foreground max-w-xs text-[13px] leading-relaxed\">\n          {t(\"emptyActivity.body\")}\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <ul className=\"flex flex-col\">\n      {events.map((e, i) => (\n        <li\n          key={`${e.kind}-${e.extensionId}-${e.at.toISOString()}-${i}`}\n          className={\n            \"flex items-center gap-3 py-3 \" +\n            (i > 0 ? \"border-border border-t\" : \"\")\n          }\n        >\n          <ActivityIcon kind={e.kind} />\n          <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n            <div className=\"text-foreground text-[13px]\">\n              <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">\n                {t(`activity.${e.kind}`)}\n              </span>{\" \"}\n              <Link\n                href={`/extensions/${e.slug}`}\n                className=\"hover:text-primary font-semibold transition-colors\"\n              >\n                {e.name}\n              </Link>\n              {e.kind === \"rated\" ? (\n                <span className=\"text-primary ml-1.5\">\n                  {\"★\".repeat(e.stars)}\n                </span>\n              ) : (\n                <span className=\"text-muted-foreground ml-1.5 font-mono text-[12px]\">\n                  {e.version}\n                </span>\n              )}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </li>\n      ))}\n    </ul>\n  );\n}\n"
}

Activity is a union of three event\n  // sources (installs, my published versions, my ratings). Each source is\n  // indexed by user — pull the most-recent N from each and merge in memory\n  // rather than a heavyweight UNION ALL.\n  const [installRows, pubRows, ratingRows] = await Promise.all([\n    db\n      .select({\n        at: installs.installedAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        version: installs.version,\n      })\n      .from(installs)\n      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, installs.extensionId))\n      .where(eq(installs.userId, userId))\n      .orderBy(desc(installs.installedAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),\n    db\n      .select({\n        at: extensionVersions.publishedAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        version: extensionVersions.version,\n      })\n      .from(extensionVersions)\n      .innerJoin(\n        extensions,\n        eq(extensions.id, extensionVersions.extensionId),\n      )\n      .where(\n        and(\n          eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n          eq(extensionVersions.status, \"ready\"),\n        ),\n      )\n      .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),\n    db\n      .select({\n        at: ratings.createdAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        stars: ratings.stars,\n      })\n      .from(ratings)\n      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, ratings.extensionId))\n      .where(eq(ratings.userId, userId))\n      .orderBy(desc(ratings.createdAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),\n  ]);\n\n  const merged: ProfileActivityEvent[] = [\n    ...installRows\n      .filter((r) => r.at != null)\n      .map(\n        (r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({\n          kind: \"installed\",\n          at: r.at as Date,\n          extensionId: r.extensionId,\n          slug: r.slug,\n          name: r.name,\n          version: r.version,\n        }),\n      ),\n    ...pubRows\n      .filter((r) => r.at != null)\n      .map(\n        (r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({\n          kind: \"published\",\n          at: r.at as Date,\n          extensionId: r.extensionId,\n          slug: r.slug,\n          name: r.name,\n          version: r.version,\n        }),\n      ),\n    ...ratingRows\n      .filter((r) => r.at != null)\n      .map(\n        (r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({\n          kind: \"rated\",\n          at: r.at as Date,\n          extensionId: r.extensionId,\n          slug: r.slug,\n          name: r.name,\n          stars: r.stars,\n        }),\n      ),\n  ];\n\n  merged.sort((a, b) => b.at.getTime() - a.at.getTime());\n  return merged.slice(0, ACTIVITY_LIMIT);\n}\n"
}

3	type ProfileHeroProps = {
4	  name: string | null;
5	  email: string;

9	
10	function initialsFor(name: string | null, email: string): string {
11	  // Guard against empty / whitespace-only names (a real DB shape — the column
12	  // is nullable and better-auth sometimes round-trips ""), and against
13	  // consecutive spaces which would otherwise insert `undefined`s.
14	  if (name && name.trim()) {
15	    return name
16	      .split(/\s+/)
17	      .filter((w) => w.length > 0)
18	      .map((w) => w[0])
19	      .join("")
20	      .slice(0, 2)
21	      .toUpperCase();
22	  }
23	  return email[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? "?";
24	}
25	
26	export function ProfileHero({
27	  name,
28	  email,

30	  deptLabel,
31	}: ProfileHeroProps) {

37	    <section className="bg-card border-border mb-6 flex items-center gap-6 rounded-2xl border px-7 py-6">

39	        aria-hidden

41	      >
42	        {initials}

45	        <h1 className="font-display text-foreground m-0 truncate text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
46	          {displayName}
47	        </h1>
48	        <div className="text-muted-foreground mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2.5 gap-y-1 text-[13px]">
49	          <span>{t("role")}</span>
50	          <span aria-hidden className="opacity-50">·</span>
51	          <span>{deptLabel ?? t("deptUnset")}</span>
52	          <span aria-hidden className="opacity-50">·</span>

54	        </div>
55	      </div>
56	    </section>
57	  );

email;\n\n  return (\n    <section className=\"bg-card border-border mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-6 rounded-2xl border px-7 py-6\">\n      <div\n        aria-hidden\n        className=\"bg-primary text-primary-foreground flex size-[72px] flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full font-display text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight shadow-[0_4px_14px_rgba(0,0,0,0.12)]\"\n      >\n        {initials}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n        <h1 className=\"font-display text-foreground m-0 truncate text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight\">\n          {displayName}\n        </h1>\n        <div className=\"text-muted-foreground mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2.5 gap-y-1 text-[13px]\">\n          <span>{t(\"role\")}</span>\n          <span aria-hidden className=\"opacity-50\">·</span>\n          <span>{deptLabel ?? t(\"deptUnset\")}</span>\n          <span aria-hidden className=\"opacity-50\">·</span>\n          <span>{joinedLabel}</span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      {stats && <HeroStats stats={stats} locale={locale} t={t} />}\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction HeroStats({\n  stats,\n  locale,\n  t,\n}: {\n  stats: ProfileHeroStats;\n  locale: string;\n  t: (key: string) => string;\n}) {\n  const fmt = new Intl.NumberFormat(locale === \"zh\" ? \"zh-CN\" : \"en-US\");\n  const items = [\n    { value: fmt.format(stats.installedCount), label: t(\"stats.installed\") },\n    { value: fmt.format(stats.publishedCount), label: t(\"stats.published\") },\n    {\n      value: fmt.format(stats.totalInstallsOfMine),\n      label: t(\"stats.installs\"),\n    },\n    {\n      value:\n        stats.avgRatingOfMine != null\n          ? stats.avgRatingOfMine.toFixed(1)\n          : \"—\",\n      label: t(\"stats.rating\"),\n      // Star suffix only when there's at least one rated extension.\n      suffix: stats.avgRatingOfMine != null ? \"★\" : null,\n    },\n  ];\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex items-center gap-7 pr-2\">\n      {items.map((it, i) => (\n        <div key={it.label} className=\"flex items-center gap-7\">\n          {i > 0 && (\n            <span aria-hidden className=\"bg-border h-9 w-px\" />\n          )}\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col items-start\">\n            <div className=\"text-foreground font-display text-2xl font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight tabular-nums\">\n              {it.value}\n              {it.suffix && (\n                <span className=\"text-primary ml-0.5\">{it.suffix}</span>\n              )}\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"text-muted-foreground mt-1.5 text-[11.5px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wider\">\n              {it.label}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}"

{

  "new_string": "  merged.sort((a, b) => b.at.getTime() - a.at.getTime());\n  return merged.slice(0, ACTIVITY_LIMIT);\n}\n\nexport type ProfileStats = {\n  installedCount: number;\n  publishedCount: number;\n  totalInstallsOfMine: number;\n  avgRatingOfMine: number | null;\n};\n\nexport async function getProfileStats(userId: string): Promise<ProfileStats> {\n  // Two small aggregates, run in parallel. Each is cheap (covered by\n  // existing indexes on installs.userId and extensions.publisherUserId).\n  // We avoid a single CTE because the failure mode (one half goes slow)\n  // is easier to diagnose split, and Postgres can parallelize anyway.\n  const [installedAgg, pubAgg] = await Promise.all([\n    db\n      .select({ c: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })\n      .from(installs)\n      .where(and(eq(installs.userId, userId), isNull(installs.uninstalledAt))),\n    db\n      .select({\n        count: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,\n        totalInstalls: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${extensions.downloadsCount}),0)::int`,\n        // Weighted average so an extension with 100 ratings counts 100×\n        // more than one with a single rating. Null when no rated\n        // extensions exist; the UI surfaces \"—\" for that case.\n        weightedStars: sql<\n          string | null\n        >`case when sum(${extensions.ratingsCount}) = 0 then null else sum(${extensions.starsAvg}::numeric * ${extensions.ratingsCount})::numeric / sum(${extensions.ratingsCount}) end`,\n      })\n      .from(extensions)\n      .where(\n        and(\n          eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n          eq(extensions.visibility, \"published\"),\n        ),\n      ),\n  ]);\n\n  const pub = pubAgg[0] ?? {\n    count: 0,\n    totalInstalls: 0,\n    weightedStars: null,\n  };\n  return {\n    installedCount: installedAgg[0]?.c ?? 0,\n    publishedCount: pub.count,\n    totalInstallsOfMine: pub.totalInstalls,\n    avgRatingOfMine:\n      pub.weightedStars != null ? Number(pub.weightedStars) : null,\n  };\n}"
}

raw to users; update the guard in the component (section-drafts.tsx) that
references r.latestStatus to also exclude any pending-prefixed statuses (e.g.,
use a check like r.latestStatus && !r.latestStatus.startsWith("pending") or
explicit checks for "pending_upload") so that pending variants are not rendered
as raw status text.

In `@lib/db/queries/profile.ts`:
- Around line 253-257: The published-activity query is missing a visibility
check: when filtering published items add a guard for the version/extension
visibility (e.g. eq(extensionVersions.visibility, "published") or the
appropriate visibility column on extensions) alongside the existing
eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId) and eq(extensionVersions.status,
"ready"); update the and(...) predicate that contains extensions.publisherUserId
and extensionVersions.status to also include the visibility eq so only truly
published items are returned.
```

> AGENT

PR #31 is CI-green (332 tests, MERGEABLE/CLEAN). CodeRabbit posted **3 actionable**, no nitpicks. My triage:

**Take #1 — `profile-hero.tsx` blank-name `<h1>`**
`displayName = name ?? email` lets `""` or whitespace render an empty heading. I already guarded `initialsFor` against this same shape in PR #30; the heading needs the same treatment. Pin with a regression test.

**Take #3 — Activity "published" query missing `visibility = "published"` guard**
A `ready` version on a `draft` extension would surface as a "published" activity event even though the extension isn't publicly visible. Real bug. Add the guard.

**Modify #2 — `section-drafts.tsx` raw status text** 
CodeRabbit's specific suggestion is wrong (`"pending_upload"` doesn't exist in the enum — actual values are `pending | scanning | ready | rejected`), but the *underlying* concern is right: showing raw `"scanning"` / `"rejected"` is ugly and untranslated. I'll translate only the actionable ones (`scanning`, `rejected`) and hide `pending`/`ready` (a `ready` draft means scanner passed and the user just hasn't clicked Publish yet — no useful status to surface). Will add i18n keys to match.

All three in one commit, push, and reply on the PR. Going.

  "new_string": "    db\n      .select({\n        at: extensionVersions.publishedAt,\n        extensionId: extensions.id,\n        slug: extensions.slug,\n        name: extensions.name,\n        version: extensionVersions.version,\n      })\n      .from(extensionVersions)\n      .innerJoin(\n        extensions,\n        eq(extensions.id, extensionVersions.extensionId),\n      )\n      // `status = ready` alone isn't enough: a ready version on a still-\n      // draft extension hasn't been published publicly yet, so it\n      // shouldn't surface as a \"published\" activity event.\n      .where(\n        and(\n          eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),\n          eq(extensions.visibility, \"published\"),\n          eq(extensionVersions.status, \"ready\"),\n        ),\n      )\n      .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt))\n      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),"
}

Recap on PR #31:

**State before fixes:** OPEN, MERGEABLE, CI green (332 tests). CodeRabbit: 3 actionable, 0 nitpicks.

**Fixes pushed (`3540bbe`):**
- `profile-hero.tsx` — blank `name` no longer renders an empty `<h1>` (now falls back to email, matching the `initialsFor` guard from PR #30). +2 regression tests.
- `lib/db/queries/profile.ts` — Activity "published" events now require `extensions.visibility = "published"`, not just `version.status = "ready"` — a ready version on a still-draft extension is invisible publicly and shouldn't show up.
- `section-drafts.tsx` — CodeRabbit's specific suggestion (`"pending_upload"`) wasn't real (actual enum: `pending | scanning | ready | rejected`); fixed the underlying issue by translating `scanning` and `rejected` via new `profile.draftStatus.*` keys and hiding the noisy `pending`/`ready` statuses. +2 tests.

**After fixes:** 336 tests green locally. CI is re-running on `3540bbe`; I'll report back when it lands.

---daily-log structure:
# AgentCenter Daily Log · 每日简报

What changed each day. For the current capability list, see [features.md](./features.md).

每日变更记录。当前已交付能力清单见 [features.md](./features.md)。

---

## English

### 2026-05-10

**Briefing.** Home page polish (banner, clickable cards, fixed trending row), new Creator + Publisher filters, production sign-up/sign-in fix, plus copy and layout cleanups.

<details>
<summary>Details</summary>

- **Home page** — Featured banner redesigned as a more polished, editorial composition (replacing the previous layout that left the right side feeling empty). The whole extension card is now clickable, not just the title. The "Trending" row used to be silently filtered to Skills only — it now shows a true cross-category mix of MCP servers, slash commands, and plugins as well, and the heading was simplified from "Trending Skills" to just "Trending". (#26, #28)

- **Browse: filter by Creator and Publisher** — Two new filters on the extensions page. Creator filters by the person who published the extension; Publisher filters by the organization behind it. A creator can belong to multiple publishers, which is reflected in the demo data. (#26)

- **Production sign-up and sign-in fix** — Registration and login were silently broken on the live site at `agentcenter.vercel.app` — requests were going nowhere. They now work on any deployment domain without extra configuration. (#27)

- **Top-bar search alignment** — The search box now sits centered in the top bar, with even space on both sides, instead of being pushed against the left. (#28)

- **Publish wizard naming** — Step 2 of the publish flow renamed from "Source / 代码来源" to "Bundle / 扩展包", which matches what the rest of the product calls it. (#28)

- **Chinese copy & typography polish** — Several wording and punctuation cleanups: a typo fix (`斜线命令` → `斜杠命令`), publisher terminology unified to `发布商` (previously a mix of `发布方` / `发布者`), proper Chinese em-dashes (`——`) replacing ASCII ones, friendlier empty-state wording, and product-name terms like `Skill` and `MCP` kept in English with proper Chinese spacing around them. (#26, #28)

- **Sidebar cleanup** — Removed an unused "Installed" entry that always showed `0`. (#26)

</details>

---

## 中文

### 2026-05-10

**简报。** 首页打磨（banner、整卡可点击、修复热门栏）、新增"作者 + 发布商"筛选、生产环境注册/登录修复，以及若干文案与布局清理。

<details>
<summary>详情</summary>

- **首页** ——精选 banner 重新设计为更精致的编辑风格构图（此前右侧大片留白显得不协调）。扩展卡片现在整体可点击，不仅仅是标题。"热门"栏此前被悄悄限制为只展示 Skill，现在能真正混合展示 MCP、斜杠命令、插件等所有类型；标题也从"热门 Skill"简化为"热门"。(#26、#28)

- **浏览：按作者和发布商筛选** ——扩展列表页新增两个筛选条件。"作者"按发布扩展的用户筛选；"发布商"按背后的组织筛选。一个作者可以属于多个发布商，这一点在示例数据里也得到了体现。(#26)

- **生产环境注册/登录修复** ——线上站点 `agentcenter.vercel.app` 上的注册和登录此前一直静默失效，请求发不出去。现在任意部署域名下都能正常使用，无需额外配置。(#27)
```

11:**Overview.** A bilingual marketplace for AI agent extensions (Skills, MCP servers, slash commands, plugins). Users can browse and search a catalog with rich filters, view detail pages with READMEs and stats, publish their own extensions through a 4-step wizard with automated bundle scanning, save favorites, and authorize a CLI for installs. Open sign-up, EN/ZH UI and content, themes, and a public registry API. Multi-tenant schema with a single-tenant UI in v1.
51:### Accounts & sign-in
55:- **Onboarding** — Pick a default department on first sign-in.
---
13:App Router, single Next.js app, server components by default. Routes are localized; everything user-facing lives behind `[locale]`. The CLI is a sibling project under `cli/` in the same monorepo (Bun workspaces).
16:/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/

349:- **Onboarding step** at `/[locale]/onboard` after first sign-up: pick your department. Required before homepage. Org membership auto-created (single-tenant for v1, but `organizations` table is populated so multi-org flip is just middleware later).
478:Separate Bun workspace package (`@agentcenter/cli`), distributed via npm (`npm i -g @agentcenter/cli`) and Homebrew (formula in P1). Single binary built with `bun build --compile` for zero-runtime install.
515:2. **CLI config** picks an active agent profile (`agentcenter config set agent claude`) and overrides paths if needed (`agentcenter config set install.basePath /custom/path`).
516:3. **Default agent profile**: `claude`. New users get the right behavior without configuration.

```markdown
## English

### 2026-05-11

**Briefing.** Personal "My Workspace" landing page — every signed-in user now has a profile hub with editable basics, four headline stats, and six tabs.

<details>
<summary>Details</summary>

- **Personal profile page** — Clicking the avatar in the top bar now reveals a new **Profile** option that opens your own workspace. The header shows your name, role, department, and the month you joined, with four numbers at a glance: how many extensions you've installed, how many you've published, total installs across your published work, and your weighted average rating. (#30, #31)

- **Editable profile basics** — Inside **Settings → Profile** you can change your display name, department, and a short bio (up to 280 characters). Email and the joined date are read-only. Save shows a confirmation; reloading the page confirms it stuck. (#30)

- **Six tabs of personal data** — *Installed* shows everything you've installed and what version is running. *Published* lists what you've shipped, with version, install count, and rating per row. *Drafts* mirrors the publish dashboard with a Continue button, and surfaces "Awaiting scan" or "Rejected" only when those statuses are relevant. *Saved* is your bookmark list. *Activity* merges installs, releases, and ratings into a single timeline (most recent twenty). Every section URL is shareable. (#30, #31)

- **Notifications and API tokens** — Both sub-tabs render a "Coming soon" placeholder while we wire them up. (#30)

</details>

---

## 中文

### 2026-05-11

**简报。** 全新的"我的工作台"个人主页——每位登录用户都有一个集合页，能直接编辑基本资料、查看四项核心数据，并在六个分页之间切换。

<details>
<summary>详情</summary>

- **个人主页** ——点击顶部头像，下拉菜单里多了一项 **Profile**，进入属于你自己的工作台。页头显示你的姓名、角色、部门和加入月份，旁边还有四个一眼可见的数字：已安装的扩展数、已发布的扩展数、你发布的扩展总安装量，以及加权平均评分。(#30、#31)

- **可编辑的资料** ——在 **设置 → 个人资料** 里可以修改昵称、部门和一段不超过 280 字的简介。邮箱和加入时间为只读。保存后会出现成功提示；刷新页面验证数据已落库。(#30)

- **六个数据分页** ——*已安装* 展示你安装过的所有扩展和当前版本号。*我发布的* 列出你发布过的扩展，每行带版本、安装量和评分。*草稿* 复用发布面板的逻辑，提供"继续"按钮，并只在"等待扫描"或"已被拒"时显示状态。*已收藏* 是书签列表。*动态* 将安装、发布、评分汇总为一条时间线（最近 20 条）。所有分页 URL 均可分享。(#30、#31)

- **通知与 API 令牌** ——两个子分页暂以"即将推出"占位，相关功能正在排期中。(#30)

</details>

---
```

### Proposed `docs/features.md` — new section under `### Save & collections`, both languages

```markdown
### Profile & workspace

- **My Workspace** — A personal landing page for every signed-in user, accessed from the avatar dropdown. Shows name, role, department, joined month, and four headline stats (installed count, published count, total installs across your published work, weighted average rating).
- **Editable profile** — Change your display name, department, and a short bio from a Settings sub-tab. Email and joined date are read-only.
- **Installed list** — Every extension you've installed, with its current version.
- **My published extensions** — Your published work with version, install count, and rating per row.
- **Drafts list** — In-progress publish drafts with a Continue button; surfaces "Awaiting scan" or "Rejected" only when relevant.
- **Saved list** — Bookmarked extensions, most recently saved first.
- **Activity feed** — Installs, releases, and ratings you've made, merged into one timeline (most recent twenty).
```

And the matching 中文 block under `### 收藏与收藏夹`.

---

1. **Routes table (line 172)** — replace `/[locale]/settings | Profile, dept, language, theme | P1` with:
   ```
   | `/[locale]/profile` | My Workspace — profile, settings, installed / published / drafts / saved / activity | **P0** |
   ```

2. **Schema snippet (~line 183-184)** — add `bio` to the `users` row.

---

180	All in `lib/db/schema/`. `withTimezone: true, defaultNow()` on every timestamp. Text PKs via `nanoid` for user-facing IDs; numeric PKs in junction tables.
181	
182	```ts
183	// schema/auth.ts — Better Auth shape
184	users: id (text pk), email (unique), emailVerified, name, image,
185	       locale ('en'|'zh' default 'en'), themePreference ('ivory'|'dark'|'system'),
186	       defaultDeptId (fk departments.id, nullable), createdAt, updatedAt
187	sessions, accounts, verifications: per Better Auth spec
188	
189	// schema/org.ts
190	organizations: id, slug (unique), name, nameZh, logoUrl, createdAt
191	departments:
192	  id (text pk, dotted-path e.g. "eng.cloud.infra"),
193	  orgId (fk organizations),
194	  parentId (fk departments.id, self-ref, nullable),
